16:     public function get_entities($request) {
17:         global $wpdb;
18:         $params = $request->get_params();
19:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
20: 
21:         $page = isset($params['page']) ? max(1, intval($params['page'])) : 1;
22:         $per_page = isset($params['per_page']) ? min(100, max(1, intval($params['per_page']))) : 20;
23:         $offset = ($page - 1) * $per_page;
24: 
25:         $where = array("1=1");
26:         $values = array();
27: 
28:         if (!empty($params['q'])) {
29:             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['q']) . '%';
30:             $where[] = "(e.name LIKE %s OR e.address_text LIKE %s)";
31:             $values[] = $like;
32:             $values[] = $like;
33:         }
34: 
35:         $where_sql = implode(' AND ', $where);
36:         $count_query = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $table e WHERE $where_sql";
37:         $total = !empty($values) ? $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare($count_query, $values)) : $wpdb->get_var($count_query);
38: 
39:         $sql = "SELECT e.*, e.name AS entity_name, e.address_text AS entity_address, e.type AS entity_type FROM $table e WHERE $where_sql ORDER BY e.name ASC LIMIT %d OFFSET %d";
40:         $query_values = array_merge($values, array($per_page, $offset));
41:         $results = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare($sql, $query_values));
42: 
43:         return rest_ensure_response(array(
44:             'data' => $results,
45:             'total' => intval($total),
46:             'page' => $page,
47:             'per_page' => $per_page,
48:             'total_pages' => ceil(intval($total) / $per_page)
49:         ));
50:     }

52:     public function create_entity($request) {
53:         global $wpdb;
54:         $params = $request->get_params();
55:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
56: 
57:         if (empty($params['entity_name'])) {
58:             return new WP_Error('missing_field', "Field 'entity_name' is required", array('status' => 400));
59:         }
60: 
61:         $objid = wp_generate_uuid4();
62:         
63:         $data = array(
64:             'objid' => $objid,
65:             'entityno' => 'ENT-' . time(), // Basic generation for now
66:             'name' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']),
67:             'entityname' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']),
68:             'address_text' => sanitize_textarea_field($params['entity_address'] ?? ''),
69:             'type' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_type'] ?? 'INDIVIDUAL'),
70:         );
71: 
72:         $result = $wpdb->insert($table, $data);
73:         if ($result === false) {
74:             return new WP_Error('insert_failed', 'Failed to create entity', array('status' => 500));
75:         }
76: 
77:         $entity = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare("SELECT e.*, e.name AS entity_name, e.address_text AS entity_address, e.type AS entity_type FROM $table e WHERE objid = %s", $objid));
78:         return rest_ensure_response($entity);
79:     }
80: 
81:     public function update_entity($id, $request) {
82:         global $wpdb;
83:         $params = $request->get_params();
84:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
85: 
86:         $data = array();
87:         if (isset($params['entity_name'])) {
88:             $data['name'] = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']);
89:             $data['entityname'] = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']);
90:         }
91:         if (isset($params['entity_address'])) {
92:             $data['address_text'] = sanitize_textarea_field($params['entity_address']);
93:         }
94:         if (isset($params['entity_type'])) {
95:             $data['type'] = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_type']);
96:         }
97: 
98:         if (empty($data)) {
99:             return new WP_Error('no_data', 'No data provided to update', array('status' => 400));
100:         }
101: 
102:         $wpdb->update($table, $data, array('objid' => $id));
103:         $entity = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare("SELECT e.*, e.name AS entity_name, e.address_text AS entity_address, e.type AS entity_type FROM $table e WHERE objid = %s", $id));
104:         return rest_ensure_response($entity);
105:     }
106: 
107:     public function delete_entity($id, $request) {
108:         global $wpdb;
109:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
110: 
111:         // Check if entity is used in any FAAS record
112:         $faas_table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
113:         $in_use = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $faas_table WHERE taxpayer_objid = %s AND state != 'DELETED'", $id));
114:         
115:         if ($in_use > 0) {
116:             return new WP_Error('entity_in_use', 'Cannot delete entity because it is used in active FAAS records', array('status' => 400));
117:         }
118: 
119:         $wpdb->delete($table, array('objid' => $id), array('%s'));
120:         return rest_ensure_response(array('success' => true, 'message' => 'Entity deleted successfully'));
121:     }

130:         $results = $wpdb->get_results("SELECT * FROM $table WHERE status = 'active' ORDER BY sort_order ASC, name ASC");
131:         return rest_ensure_response($results);

138:     public function get_faas_list($request) {
139:         global $wpdb;
140:         $params = $request->get_params();
141: 
142:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
143:         $t_rpu = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_rpu';
144:         $t_rp = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_real_property';
145:         $t_entity = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
146:         $t_txn = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas_txntypes';
147: 
148:         $page = isset($params['page']) ? max(1, intval($params['page'])) : 1;
149:         $per_page = isset($params['per_page']) ? min(100, max(1, intval($params['per_page']))) : 20;
150:         $offset = ($page - 1) * $per_page;
151: 
152:         $where = array("f.state != 'DELETED'");
153:         $values = array();
154: 
155:         // Free-text search
156:         if (!empty($params['q'])) {
157:             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['q']) . '%';
158:             $where[] = "(f.tdno LIKE %s OR f.owner_name LIKE %s OR e.name LIKE %s OR rp.cadastral_lot_no LIKE %s OR f.fullpin LIKE %s)";
159:             $values = array_merge($values, array($like, $like, $like, $like, $like));
160:         }
161: 
162:         // Specific filters
163:         if (!empty($params['state'])) {
164:             $where[] = "f.state = %s";
165:             $values[] = strtoupper($params['state']);
166:         }
167:         if (!empty($params['rpu_type'])) {
168:             $where[] = "r.rpu_type = %s";
169:             $values[] = strtoupper($params['rpu_type']);
170:         }
171:         if (!empty($params['txntype'])) {
172:             $where[] = "f.txntype_objid = %s";
173:             $values[] = $params['txntype'];
174:         }
175:         if (!empty($params['barangay'])) {
176:             $where[] = "rp.barangay LIKE %s";
177:             $values[] = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['barangay']) . '%';
178:         }
179:         if (!empty($params['classification'])) {
180:             $where[] = "r.classification = %s";
181:             $values[] = $params['classification'];
182:         }
183:         if (!empty($params['tdno'])) {
184:             $where[] = "f.tdno LIKE %s";
185:             $values[] = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['tdno']) . '%';
186:         }
187:         if (!empty($params['owner_name'])) {
188:             $where[] = "(f.owner_name LIKE %s OR e.name LIKE %s)";
189:             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['owner_name']) . '%';
190:             $values[] = $like;
191:             $values[] = $like;
192:         }
193: 
194:         $where_sql = implode(' AND ', $where);
195: 
196:         $joins = "
197:             LEFT JOIN $t_rpu r ON f.rpuid = r.id
198:             LEFT JOIN $t_rp rp ON f.realpropertyid = rp.id
199:             LEFT JOIN $t_entity e ON f.taxpayer_objid = e.objid
200:             LEFT JOIN $t_txn tx ON f.txntype_objid = tx.code
201:         ";
202: 
203:         // Count
204:         $count_sql = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t_faas f $joins WHERE $where_sql";
205:         $total = !empty($values) ? $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare($count_sql, $values)) : $wpdb->get_var($count_sql);
206: 
207:         // Build ORDER BY
208:         $order_by = 'f.txntimestamp DESC';
209:         if (!empty($params['order_by'])) {
210:             $allowed = array('tdno', 'owner_name', 'total_assessed_value', 'created_at', 'effectivity_year', 'txntimestamp');
211:             $field = $params['order_by'];
212:             if ($field === 'created_at') $field = 'txntimestamp';
213:             
214:             if (in_array($field, $allowed)) {
215:                 $dir = (!empty($params['order_direction']) && strtoupper($params['order_direction']) === 'ASC') ? 'ASC' : 'DESC';
216:                 if ($field === 'total_assessed_value') {
217:                     $order_by = "r.$field $dir";
218:                 } else {
219:                     $order_by = "f.$field $dir";
220:                 }
221:             }
222:         }
223: 
224:         $select_sql = "
225:             SELECT
226:                 f.*,
227:                 f.objid AS id,
228:                 f.rpuid AS rpu_id,
229:                 f.realpropertyid AS real_property_id,
230:                 f.taxpayer_objid AS taxpayer_id,
231:                 f.effectivityyear AS effectivity_year,
232:                 f.effectivityqtr AS effectivity_qtr,
233:                 f.txntype_objid AS txntype_code,
234:                 f.titletype AS title_type,
235:                 f.titleno AS title_no,
236:                 f.titledate AS title_date,
237:                 r.rpu_type,
238:                 r.classification,
239:                 r.total_market_value,
240:                 r.total_assessed_value,
241:                 r.ry AS revision_year,
242:                 rp.pin AS rp_pin,
243:                 rp.cadastral_lot_no,
244:                 rp.survey_no,
245:                 rp.block_no,
246:                 rp.barangay,
247:                 rp.total_area_hectare,
248:                 rp.total_area_sqm,
249:                 e.name AS taxpayer_name,
250:                 e.address_text AS taxpayer_address,
251:                 tx.name AS txntype_name
252:             FROM $t_faas f
253:             $joins
254:             WHERE $where_sql
255:             ORDER BY $order_by
256:             LIMIT %d OFFSET %d
257:         ";
258: 
259:         $query_values = array_merge($values, array($per_page, $offset));
260:         $results = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare($select_sql, $query_values));
261: 
262:         return rest_ensure_response(array(
263:             'data' => $results,
264:             'total' => intval($total),
265:             'page' => $page,
266:             'per_page' => $per_page,
267:             'total_pages' => ceil(intval($total) / $per_page)
268:         ));
269:     }

272:     // FAAS SINGLE RECORD
273:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
274: 
275:     public function get_faas($id) {
276:         global $wpdb;
277: 
278:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
279:         $t_rpu = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_rpu';
280:         $t_rp = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_real_property';
281:         $t_entity = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
282:         $t_txn = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas_txntypes';
283: 
284:         $sql = "
285:             SELECT
286:                 f.*,
287:                 f.objid AS id,
288:                 f.rpuid AS rpu_id,
289:                 f.realpropertyid AS real_property_id,
290:                 f.taxpayer_objid AS taxpayer_id,
291:                 f.effectivityyear AS effectivity_year,
292:                 f.effectivityqtr AS effectivity_qtr,
293:                 f.txntype_objid AS txntype_code,
294:                 f.titletype AS title_type,
295:                 f.titleno AS title_no,
296:                 f.titledate AS title_date,
297:                 r.rpu_type,
298:                 r.classification,
299:                 r.total_market_value,
300:                 r.total_assessed_value,
301:                 r.ry AS revision_year,
302:                 rp.pin AS rp_pin,
303:                 rp.cadastral_lot_no,
304:                 rp.survey_no,
305:                 rp.block_no,
306:                 rp.barangay,
307:                 rp.total_area_hectare,
308:                 rp.total_area_sqm,
309:                 rp.north,
310:                 rp.south,
311:                 rp.east,
312:                 rp.west,
313:                 e.name AS taxpayer_name,
314:                 e.address_text AS taxpayer_address,
315:                 e.type AS taxpayer_type,
316:                 tx.name AS txntype_name
317:             FROM $t_faas f
318:             LEFT JOIN $t_rpu r ON f.rpuid = r.id
319:             LEFT JOIN $t_rp rp ON f.realpropertyid = rp.id
320:             LEFT JOIN $t_entity e ON f.taxpayer_objid = e.objid
321:             LEFT JOIN $t_txn tx ON f.txntype_objid = tx.code
322:             WHERE f.objid = %s
323:         ";
324: 
325:         $record = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare($sql, $id));
326:         if (!$record) {
327:             return new WP_Error('not_found', 'FAAS record not found', array('status' => 404));
328:         }
329: 
330:         return $record;
331:     }

334:     // FAAS CREATE
335:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
336: 
337:     public function create_faas($request) {
338:         global $wpdb;
339:         $params = $request->get_params();
340:         $auth = new Assessor_Auth();
341:         $user_id = $auth->get_user_id_from_token($request);
342: 
343:         // Validate required
344:         $required = array('tdno', 'owner_name');
345:         foreach ($required as $field) {
346:             if (empty($params[$field])) {
347:                 return new WP_Error('missing_field', "Field '$field' is required", array('status' => 400));
348:             }
349:         }
350: 
351:         // Check duplicate TD
352:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
353:         $existing = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
354:             "SELECT objid FROM $t_faas WHERE tdno = %s AND state != 'DELETED'",
355:             $params['tdno']
356:         ));
357:         if ($existing) {
358:             return new WP_Error('duplicate_tdno', 'Tax Declaration Number already exists', array('status' => 400));
359:         }
360: 
361:         // 1. Upsert Entity (taxpayer)
362:         $taxpayer_id = $this->upsert_entity($params);
363: 
364:         // 2. Create Real Property
365:         $real_property_id = $this->create_real_property($params);
366: 
367:         // 3. Create RPU
368:         $rpu_id = $this->create_rpu($params, $real_property_id);
369: 
370:         // 4. Create FAAS
371:         $faas_objid = wp_generate_uuid4();
372:         $faas_data = array(
373:             'objid'                 => $faas_objid,
374:             'state'                 => 'CURRENT',
375:             'rpuid'                 => $rpu_id,
376:             'realpropertyid'        => $real_property_id,
377:             'taxpayer_objid'        => $taxpayer_id,
378:             'tdno'                  => sanitize_text_field($params['tdno']),
379:             'utdno'                 => !empty($params['utdno']) ? sanitize_text_field($params['utdno']) : sanitize_text_field($params['tdno']),
380:             'txntype_objid'         => sanitize_text_field($params['txntype_code'] ?? 'GR'),
381:             'effectivityyear'       => intval($params['effectivity_year'] ?? 0),
382:             'effectivityqtr'        => intval($params['effectivity_qtr'] ?? 0),
383:             'owner_name'            => sanitize_text_field($params['owner_name']),
384:             'owner_address'         => sanitize_textarea_field($params['owner_address'] ?? ''),
385:             'administrator_name'    => sanitize_text_field($params['administrator_name'] ?? ''),
386:             'administrator_address' => sanitize_text_field($params['administrator_address'] ?? ''),
387:             'beneficiary_name'      => sanitize_text_field($params['beneficiary_name'] ?? ''),
388:             'beneficiary_address'   => sanitize_text_field($params['beneficiary_address'] ?? ''),
389:             'fullpin'               => sanitize_text_field($params['pin'] ?? ''),
390:             'titletype'             => sanitize_text_field($params['title_type'] ?? ''),
391:             'titleno'               => sanitize_text_field($params['title_no'] ?? ''),
392:             'titledate'             => !empty($params['title_date']) ? $params['title_date'] : null,
393:             'prevtdno'              => sanitize_text_field($params['prevtdno'] ?? ''),
394:             'prevowner'             => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_owner'] ?? ''),
395:             'prevav'                => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_assessed_value'] ?? ''),
396:             'prevmv'                => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_market_value'] ?? ''),
397:             'prevareaha'            => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_area_hectare'] ?? ''),
398:             'prevareasqm'           => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_area_sqm'] ?? ''),
399:             'preveffectivity'       => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_effectivity'] ?? ''),
400:             'memoranda'             => sanitize_textarea_field($params['memoranda'] ?? ''),
401:             'backtaxyrs'            => intval($params['back_tax_years'] ?? 0),
402:             'ryordinanceno'         => sanitize_text_field($params['ry_ordinance_no'] ?? ''),
403:             'ryordinancedate'       => !empty($params['ry_ordinance_date']) ? $params['ry_ordinance_date'] : null,
404:             'dtapproved'            => !empty($params['date_approved']) ? $params['date_approved'] : null,
405:             'year'                  => !empty($params['year_issued']) ? intval($params['year_issued']) : null,
406:             'publicland'            => intval($params['public_land'] ?? 0),
407:             'txntimestamp'          => current_time('mysql')
408:         );
409: 
410:         $result = $wpdb->insert($t_faas, $faas_data);
411:         if ($result === false) {
412:             return new WP_Error('insert_failed', 'Failed to create FAAS record: ' . $wpdb->last_error, array('status' => 500));
413:         }
414: 
415:         // Cancel previous TD if provided
416:         if (!empty($params['prevtdno'])) {
417:             $prev_td = sanitize_text_field($params['prevtdno']);
418:             $wpdb->query($wpdb->prepare(
419:                 "UPDATE $t_faas SET state = 'CANCELLED', canceldate = %s, cancelledtimestamp = %s, cancelledbytdnos = %s WHERE tdno = %s AND state != 'CANCELLED'",
420:                 current_time('mysql'),
421:                 current_time('mysql'),
422:                 $faas_data['tdno'],
423:                 $prev_td
424:             ));
425:         }
426: 
427:         return rest_ensure_response($this->get_faas($faas_objid));
428:     }
429: 
430:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
431:     // FAAS UPDATE
432:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
433: 
434:     public function update_faas($id, $request) {
435:         global $wpdb;
436:         $params = $request->get_params();
437:         $auth = new Assessor_Auth();
438:         $user_id = $auth->get_user_id_from_token($request);
439: 
440:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
441:         $current = $this->get_faas($id);
442:         if (is_wp_error($current)) return $current;
443: 
444:         // Check duplicate TD if changing
445:         if (isset($params['tdno'])) {
446:             $new_td = sanitize_text_field($params['tdno']);
447:             $dup = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
448:                 "SELECT objid FROM $t_faas WHERE tdno = %s AND objid != %s AND state != 'DELETED'",
449:                 $new_td,
450:                 $id
451:             ));
452:             if ($dup) {
453:                 return new WP_Error('duplicate_tdno', 'Tax Declaration Number already exists', array('status' => 400));
454:             }
455:         }
456: 
457:         // Update Entity if name/address changed
458:         if (isset($params['owner_name']) || isset($params['owner_address'])) {
459:             $this->upsert_entity($params, $current->taxpayer_id);
460:         }
461: 
462:         // Update Real Property
463:         if ($current->real_property_id) {
464:             $this->update_real_property($current->real_property_id, $params);
465:         }
466: 
467:         // Update RPU
468:         if ($current->rpu_id) {
469:             $this->update_rpu($current->rpu_id, $params);
470:         }
471: 
472:         // Map frontend fields to DB columns
473:         $field_map = array(
474:             'tdno'                  => 'tdno',
475:             'utdno'                 => 'utdno',
476:             'txntype_code'          => 'txntype_objid',
477:             'effectivity_year'      => 'effectivityyear',
478:             'effectivity_qtr'       => 'effectivityqtr',
479:             'owner_name'            => 'owner_name',
480:             'owner_address'         => 'owner_address',
481:             'administrator_name'    => 'administrator_name',
482:             'administrator_address' => 'administrator_address',
483:             'beneficiary_name'      => 'beneficiary_name',
484:             'beneficiary_address'   => 'beneficiary_address',
485:             'pin'                   => 'fullpin',
486:             'title_type'            => 'titletype',
487:             'title_no'              => 'titleno',
488:             'title_date'            => 'titledate',
489:             'prevtdno'              => 'prevtdno',
490:             'prev_owner'            => 'prevowner',
491:             'prev_assessed_value'   => 'prevav',
492:             'prev_market_value'     => 'prevmv',
493:             'prev_area_hectare'     => 'prevareaha',
494:             'prev_area_sqm'         => 'prevareasqm',
495:             'prev_effectivity'      => 'preveffectivity',
496:             'memoranda'             => 'memoranda',
497:             'back_tax_years'        => 'backtaxyrs',
498:             'ry_ordinance_no'       => 'ryordinanceno',
499:             'ry_ordinance_date'     => 'ryordinancedate',
500:             'date_approved'         => 'dtapproved',
501:             'year_issued'           => 'year',
502:             'public_land'           => 'publicland',
503:             'state'                 => 'state',
504:             'cancel_reason'         => 'cancelreason',
505:             'cancel_note'           => 'cancelnote',
506:             'cancel_date'           => 'canceldate',
507:             'cancelled_by_tdnos'    => 'cancelledbytdnos'
508:         );
509: 
510:         $update_data = array();
511:         // Since ETRACS schema doesn't have an updated_by column, skip it.
512:         // Or if it does, add it. The schema given by user does not have updated_by.
513: 
514:         foreach ($field_map as $param_key => $db_col) {
515:             if (!array_key_exists($param_key, $params)) continue;
516:             
517:             $val = $params[$param_key];
518:             
519:             if (in_array($param_key, array('effectivity_year', 'effectivity_qtr', 'back_tax_years', 'public_land', 'year_issued'))) {
520:                 $update_data[$db_col] = !empty($val) ? intval($val) : 0;
521:             } elseif (in_array($param_key, array('title_date', 'ry_ordinance_date', 'date_approved', 'cancel_date'))) {
522:                 $update_data[$db_col] = !empty($val) ? $val : null;
523:             } else {
524:                 $update_data[$db_col] = sanitize_text_field($val);
525:             }
526:         }
527: 
528:         if (isset($update_data['utdno']) && empty($update_data['utdno'])) {
529:             $update_data['utdno'] = $update_data['tdno'] ?? $current->tdno;
530:         }
531: 
532:         $wpdb->update($t_faas, $update_data, array('objid' => $id));
533: 
534:         // Cancel previous TD if provided/updated
535:         if (!empty($params['prevtdno'])) {
536:             $prev_td = sanitize_text_field($params['prevtdno']);
537:             $new_td = $update_data['tdno'] ?? $current->tdno;
538:             $wpdb->query($wpdb->prepare(
539:                 "UPDATE $t_faas SET state = 'CANCELLED', canceldate = %s, cancelledtimestamp = %s, cancelledbytdnos = %s WHERE tdno = %s AND state != 'CANCELLED'",
540:                 current_time('mysql'),
541:                 current_time('mysql'),
542:                 $new_td,
543:                 $prev_td
544:             ));
545:         }
546: 
547:         return rest_ensure_response($this->get_faas($id));
548:     }
549: 
550:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
551:     // FAAS DELETE (soft delete → DELETED state)
552:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
553: 
554:     public function delete_faas($id, $request) {
555:         global $wpdb;
556:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
557: 
558:         $current = $this->get_faas($id);
559:         if (is_wp_error($current)) return $current;
560: 
561:         $wpdb->update($t_faas, array('state' => 'DELETED'), array('objid' => $id));
562: 
563:         return rest_ensure_response(array('success' => true, 'message' => 'FAAS record deleted'));
564:     }
565: 
566:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
567:     // FAAS CANCEL
568:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
569: 
570:     public function cancel_faas($id, $request) {
571:         global $wpdb;
572:         $params = $request->get_params();
573:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
574: 
575:         $current = $this->get_faas($id);
576:         if (is_wp_error($current)) return $current;
577: 
578:         if ($current->state !== 'CURRENT') {
579:             return new WP_Error('invalid_state', 'Only CURRENT records can be cancelled', array('status' => 400));
580:         }
581: 
582:         $update = array(
583:             'state'             => 'CANCELLED',
584:             'cancelreason'      => sanitize_text_field($params['cancel_reason'] ?? ''),
585:             'canceldate'        => !empty($params['cancel_date']) ? $params['cancel_date'] : current_time('mysql'),
586:             'cancelnote'        => sanitize_text_field($params['cancel_note'] ?? ''),
587:             'cancelledbytdnos'  => sanitize_text_field($params['cancelled_by_tdnos'] ?? ''),
588:             'cancelledtimestamp'=> current_time('mysql'),
589:         );
590: 
591:         $wpdb->update($t_faas, $update, array('objid' => $id));
592:         return rest_ensure_response($this->get_faas($id));
593:     }

599:     public function get_faas_stats($request) {
600:         global $wpdb;
601:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
602:         $t_rpu = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_rpu';
603: 
604:         $total = $wpdb->get_var("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t_faas WHERE state != 'DELETED'");
605:         $current = $wpdb->get_var("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t_faas WHERE state = 'CURRENT'");
606:         $cancelled = $wpdb->get_var("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t_faas WHERE state = 'CANCELLED'");
607: 
608:         $by_type = $wpdb->get_results("
609:             SELECT r.rpu_type, COUNT(*) as count
610:             FROM $t_faas f
611:             LEFT JOIN $t_rpu r ON f.rpuid = r.id
612:             WHERE f.state != 'DELETED'
613:             GROUP BY r.rpu_type
614:         ");
615: 
616:         return rest_ensure_response(array(
617:             'total' => intval($total),
618:             'current' => intval($current),
619:             'cancelled' => intval($cancelled),
620:             'by_type' => $by_type
621:         ));
622:     }

628:     private function upsert_entity($params, $existing_id = null) {
629:         global $wpdb;
630:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
631: 
632:         $name = sanitize_text_field($params['owner_name'] ?? $params['entity_name'] ?? '');
633:         $address = sanitize_textarea_field($params['owner_address'] ?? $params['entity_address'] ?? '');
634:         $type = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_type'] ?? 'INDIVIDUAL');
635: 
636:         if ($existing_id) {
637:             $wpdb->update($table, array(
638:                 'name' => $name,
639:                 'entityname' => $name,
640:                 'address_text' => $address,
641:             ), array('objid' => $existing_id));
642:             return $existing_id;
643:         }
644: 
645:         // Try to find by exact name match
646:         $found = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
647:             "SELECT objid FROM $table WHERE entityname = %s LIMIT 1",
648:             $name
649:         ));
650:         if ($found) return $found;
651: 
652:         // Create new
653:         $objid = wp_generate_uuid4();
654:         $wpdb->insert($table, array(
655:             'objid' => $objid,
656:             'entityno' => 'ENT-' . time(),
657:             'name' => $name,
658:             'entityname' => $name,
659:             'address_text' => $address,
660:             'type' => $type,
661:         ));
662: 
663:         return $objid;
664:     }

673:             'survey_no'        => sanitize_text_field($params['survey_no'] ?? ''),
674:             'block_no'         => sanitize_text_field($params['block_no'] ?? ''),
675:             'barangay'         => sanitize_text_field($params['barangay'] ?? ''),
676:             'municipality'     => sanitize_text_field($params['municipality'] ?? ''),
677:             'province'         => sanitize_text_field($params['province'] ?? ''),
678:             'total_area_hectare' => isset($params['total_area_hectare']) && $params['total_area_hectare'] !== '' ? floatval($params['total_area_hectare']) : null,
679:             'total_area_sqm'     => isset($params['total_area_sqm']) && $params['total_area_sqm'] !== '' ? floatval($params['total_area_sqm']) : null,
680:             'north'            => sanitize_text_field($params['north'] ?? ''),
681:             'south'            => sanitize_text_field($params['south'] ?? ''),
682:             'east'             => sanitize_text_field($params['east'] ?? ''),
683:             'west'             => sanitize_text_field($params['west'] ?? ''),
684:         );

690:     private function update_real_property($id, $params) {
691:         global $wpdb;
692:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_real_property';
693: 
694:         $fields = array('pin', 'cadastral_lot_no', 'survey_no', 'block_no', 'barangay', 'municipality', 'province', 'north', 'south', 'east', 'west');
695:         $update = array();
696:         foreach ($fields as $f) {
697:             // Map alternative field names
698:             $param_key = $f;
699:             if ($f === 'cadastral_lot_no' && !isset($params[$f]) && isset($params['lot_number'])) $param_key = 'lot_number';
700:             if (isset($params[$param_key])) {
701:                 $update[$f] = sanitize_text_field($params[$param_key]);
702:             }
703:         }
704:         // Numeric area fields
705:         foreach (array('total_area_hectare', 'total_area_sqm') as $af) {
706:             if (array_key_exists($af, $params)) {
707:                 $update[$af] = $params[$af] !== '' && $params[$af] !== null ? floatval($params[$af]) : null;
708:             }
709:         }
710: 
711:         if (!empty($update)) {
712:             $wpdb->update($table, $update, array('id' => $id));
713:         }
714:     }
715: 
716:     private function create_rpu($params, $real_property_id) {

718:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_rpu';

720:         $data = array(
721:             'real_property_id'     => $real_property_id,
722:             'rpu_type'             => strtoupper(sanitize_text_field($params['rpu_type'] ?? 'LAND')),
723:             'classification'       => sanitize_text_field($params['classification'] ?? ''),
724:             'ry'                   => intval($params['ry'] ?? $params['revision_year'] ?? 0),
725:             'total_market_value'   => isset($params['total_market_value']) && $params['total_market_value'] !== '' ? floatval($params['total_market_value']) : 0,
726:             'total_assessed_value' => isset($params['total_assessed_value']) && $params['total_assessed_value'] !== '' ? floatval($params['total_assessed_value']) : 0,
727:             'taxable'              => intval($params['taxable'] ?? 1),
728:         );
729: 
730:         $wpdb->insert($table, $data);
731:         return $wpdb->insert_id;
732:     }

734:     private function update_rpu($id, $params) {
735:         global $wpdb;
736:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_rpu';
737: 
738:         $update = array();
739:         if (isset($params['rpu_type'])) $update['rpu_type'] = strtoupper(sanitize_text_field($params['rpu_type']));
740:         if (isset($params['classification'])) $update['classification'] = sanitize_text_field($params['classification']);
741:         if (isset($params['ry']) || isset($params['revision_year'])) $update['ry'] = intval($params['ry'] ?? $params['revision_year']);
742:         if (array_key_exists('total_market_value', $params)) $update['total_market_value'] = floatval($params['total_market_value']);
743:         if (array_key_exists('total_assessed_value', $params)) $update['total_assessed_value'] = floatval($params['total_assessed_value']);
744:         if (isset($params['taxable'])) $update['taxable'] = intval($params['taxable']);
745: 
746:         if (!empty($update)) {
747:             $wpdb->update($table, $update, array('id' => $id));
748:         }
749:     }

3: /**
4:  * Assessor ETRACS Properties Module
5:  * 
6:  * Manages ETRACS-modeled FAAS (Field Appraisal & Assessment Sheet) records
7:  * with separate Entity, Real Property, and RPU tables following the
8:  * ETRACS 2.5.4 database structure.
9:  */
10: class Assessor_Etracs {

12:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
13:     // ENTITIES (Taxpayer / Owner)

16:     public function get_entities($request) {
17:         global $wpdb;
18:         $params = $request->get_params();
19:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
20: 
21:         $page = isset($params['page']) ? max(1, intval($params['page'])) : 1;
22:         $per_page = isset($params['per_page']) ? min(100, max(1, intval($params['per_page']))) : 20;
23:         $offset = ($page - 1) * $per_page;
24: 
25:         $where = array("1=1");
26:         $values = array();
27: 
28:         if (!empty($params['q'])) {
29:             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['q']) . '%';
30:             $where[] = "(e.name LIKE %s OR e.address_text LIKE %s)";
31:             $values[] = $like;
32:             $values[] = $like;
33:         }
34: 
35:         $where_sql = implode(' AND ', $where);
36:         $count_query = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $table e WHERE $where_sql";
37:         $total = !empty($values) ? $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare($count_query, $values)) : $wpdb->get_var($count_query);
38: 
39:         $sql = "SELECT e.*, e.name AS entity_name, e.address_text AS entity_address, e.type AS entity_type FROM $table e WHERE $where_sql ORDER BY e.name ASC LIMIT %d OFFSET %d";
40:         $query_values = array_merge($values, array($per_page, $offset));
41:         $results = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare($sql, $query_values));
42: 
43:         return rest_ensure_response(array(
44:             'data' => $results,
45:             'total' => intval($total),
46:             'page' => $page,
47:             'per_page' => $per_page,
48:             'total_pages' => ceil(intval($total) / $per_page)
49:         ));
50:     }
51: 
52:     public function create_entity($request) {
53:         global $wpdb;

55:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
56:

59:         }
60:

67:             'entityname' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']),
68:             'address_text' => sanitize_textarea_field($params['entity_address'] ?? ''),
69:             'type' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_type'] ?? 'INDIVIDUAL'),
70:         );
71: 
72:         $result = $wpdb->insert($table, $data);
73:         if ($result === false) {
74:             return new WP_Error('insert_failed', 'Failed to create entity', array('status' => 500));

76:

78:         return rest_ensure_response($entity);

336: 
337:     public function create_faas($request) {
338:         global $wpdb;
339:         $params = $request->get_params();
340:         $auth = new Assessor_Auth();
341:         $user_id = $auth->get_user_id_from_token($request);
342: 
343:         // Validate required
344:         $required = array('tdno', 'owner_name');
345:         foreach ($required as $field) {
346:             if (empty($params[$field])) {
347:                 return new WP_Error('missing_field', "Field '$field' is required", array('status' => 400));
348:             }
349:         }
350: 
351:         // Check duplicate TD
352:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
353:         $existing = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
354:             "SELECT objid FROM $t_faas WHERE tdno = %s AND state != 'DELETED'",
355:             $params['tdno']
356:         ));
357:         if ($existing) {
358:             return new WP_Error('duplicate_tdno', 'Tax Declaration Number already exists', array('status' => 400));
359:         }
360: 
361:         // 1. Upsert Entity (taxpayer)
362:         $taxpayer_id = $this->upsert_entity($params);
363: 
364:         // 2. Create Real Property
365:         $real_property_id = $this->create_real_property($params);
366: 
367:         // 3. Create RPU
368:         $rpu_id = $this->create_rpu($params, $real_property_id);
369: 
370:         // 4. Create FAAS
371:         $faas_objid = wp_generate_uuid4();
372:         $faas_data = array(
373:             'objid'                 => $faas_objid,
374:             'state'                 => 'CURRENT',
375:             'rpuid'                 => $rpu_id,
376:             'realpropertyid'        => $real_property_id,
377:             'taxpayer_objid'        => $taxpayer_id,
378:             'tdno'                  => sanitize_text_field($params['tdno']),
379:             'utdno'                 => !empty($params['utdno']) ? sanitize_text_field($params['utdno']) : sanitize_text_field($params['tdno']),
380:             'txntype_objid'         => sanitize_text_field($params['txntype_code'] ?? 'GR'),
381:             'effectivityyear'       => intval($params['effectivity_year'] ?? 0),
382:             'effectivityqtr'        => intval($params['effectivity_qtr'] ?? 0),
383:             'owner_name'            => sanitize_text_field($params['owner_name']),
384:             'owner_address'         => sanitize_textarea_field($params['owner_address'] ?? ''),
385:             'administrator_name'    => sanitize_text_field($params['administrator_name'] ?? ''),

495:         $field_map = array(
496:             'tdno'                  => 'tdno',
497:             'utdno'                 => 'utdno',
498:             'txntype_code'          => 'txntype_objid',
499:             'effectivity_year'      => 'effectivityyear',

3: /**
4:  * Assessor ETRACS Properties Module
5:  * 
6:  * Manages ETRACS-modeled FAAS (Field Appraisal & Assessment Sheet) records
7:  * with separate Entity, Real Property, and RPU tables following the

12:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
13:     // ENTITIES (Taxpayer / Owner)
14:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
15: 
16:     public function get_entities($request) {
17:         global $wpdb;
18:         $params = $request->get_params();
19:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
20: 
21:         $page = isset($params['page']) ? max(1, intval($params['page'])) : 1;
22:         $per_page = isset($params['per_page']) ? min(100, max(1, intval($params['per_page']))) : 20;
23:         $offset = ($page - 1) * $per_page;
24: 
25:         $where = array("1=1");
26:         $values = array();
27: 
28:         if (!empty($params['q'])) {
29:             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['q']) . '%';
30:             $where[] = "(e.name LIKE %s OR e.address_text LIKE %s)";
31:             $values[] = $like;
32:             $values[] = $like;
33:         }
34: 
35:         $where_sql = implode(' AND ', $where);
36:         $count_query = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $table e WHERE $where_sql";
37:         $total = !empty($values) ? $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare($count_query, $values)) : $wpdb->get_var($count_query);
38: 
39:         $sql = "SELECT e.*, e.name AS entity_name, e.address_text AS entity_address, e.type AS entity_type FROM $table e WHERE $where_sql ORDER BY e.name ASC LIMIT %d OFFSET %d";
40:         $query_values = array_merge($values, array($per_page, $offset));
41:         $results = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare($sql, $query_values));
42: 
43:         return rest_ensure_response(array(
44:             'data' => $results,
45:             'total' => intval($total),
46:             'page' => $page,
47:             'per_page' => $per_page,
48:             'total_pages' => ceil(intval($total) / $per_page)
49:         ));
50:     }

52:     public function create_entity($request) {
53:         global $wpdb;
54:         $params = $request->get_params();
55:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
56: 
57:         if (empty($params['entity_name'])) {
58:             return new WP_Error('missing_field', "Field 'entity_name' is required", array('status' => 400));
59:         }
60: 
61:         $objid = wp_generate_uuid4();
62:         
63:         $data = array(
64:             'objid' => $objid,
65:             'entityno' => 'ENT-' . time(), // Basic generation for now
66:             'name' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']),
67:             'entityname' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']),
68:             'address_text' => sanitize_textarea_field($params['entity_address'] ?? ''),
69:             'type' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_type'] ?? 'INDIVIDUAL'),
70:         );
71: 
72:         $result = $wpdb->insert($table, $data);
73:         if ($result === false) {
74:             return new WP_Error('insert_failed', 'Failed to create entity', array('status' => 500));
75:         }
76: 
77:         $entity = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare("SELECT e.*, e.name AS entity_name, e.address_text AS entity_address, e.type AS entity_type FROM $table e WHERE objid = %s", $objid));
78:         return rest_ensure_response($entity);
79:     }
80: 
81:     public function update_entity($id, $request) {
82:         global $wpdb;
83:         $params = $request->get_params();
84:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
85: 
86:         $data = array();
87:         if (isset($params['entity_name'])) {
88:             $data['name'] = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']);
89:             $data['entityname'] = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']);
90:         }
91:         if (isset($params['entity_address'])) {
92:             $data['address_text'] = sanitize_textarea_field($params['entity_address']);
93:         }
94:         if (isset($params['entity_type'])) {
95:             $data['type'] = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_type']);
96:         }
97: 
98:         if (empty($data)) {
99:             return new WP_Error('no_data', 'No data provided to update', array('status' => 400));
100:         }
101: 
102:         $wpdb->update($table, $data, array('objid' => $id));
103:         $entity = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare("SELECT e.*, e.name AS entity_name, e.address_text AS entity_address, e.type AS entity_type FROM $table e WHERE objid = %s", $id));
104:         return rest_ensure_response($entity);
105:     }

107:     public function delete_entity($id, $request) {
108:         global $wpdb;
109:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';

163:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
164:     // FAAS LISTING (main list view)
165:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
166: 
167:     public function get_faas_list($request) {
168:         global $wpdb;
169:         $params = $request->get_params();
170: 
171:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
172:         $t_rpu = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_rpu';
173:         $t_rp = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_real_property';
174:         $t_entity = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
175:         $t_txn = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas_txntypes';
176: 
177:         $page = isset($params['page']) ? max(1, intval($params['page'])) : 1;
178:         $per_page = isset($params['per_page']) ? min(100, max(1, intval($params['per_page']))) : 20;
179:         $offset = ($page - 1) * $per_page;
180: 
181:         $where = array("f.state != 'DELETED'");
182:         $values = array();
183: 
184:         // Free-text search
185:         if (!empty($params['q'])) {
186:             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['q']) . '%';
187:             $where[] = "(f.tdno LIKE %s OR f.owner_name LIKE %s OR e.name LIKE %s OR rp.cadastral_lot_no LIKE %s OR f.fullpin LIKE %s)";
188:             $values = array_merge($values, array($like, $like, $like, $like, $like));
189:         }
190: 
191:         // Specific filters
192:         if (!empty($params['state'])) {
193:             $where[] = "f.state = %s";
194:             $values[] = strtoupper($params['state']);
195:         }
196:         if (!empty($params['rpu_type'])) {
197:             $where[] = "r.rpu_type = %s";
198:             $values[] = strtoupper($params['rpu_type']);
199:         }
200:         if (!empty($params['txntype'])) {
201:             $where[] = "f.txntype_objid = %s";
202:             $values[] = $params['txntype'];
203:         }
204:         if (!empty($params['barangay'])) {
205:             $where[] = "rp.barangay LIKE %s";
206:             $values[] = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['barangay']) . '%';
207:         }
208:         if (!empty($params['classification'])) {
209:             $where[] = "r.classification = %s";
210:             $values[] = $params['classification'];
211:         }
212:         if (!empty($params['tdno'])) {
213:             $where[] = "f.tdno LIKE %s";
214:             $values[] = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['tdno']) . '%';
215:         }
216:         if (!empty($params['owner_name'])) {
217:             $where[] = "(f.owner_name LIKE %s OR e.name LIKE %s)";
218:             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['owner_name']) . '%';
219:             $values[] = $like;
220:             $values[] = $like;
221:         }
222: 
223:         $where_sql = implode(' AND ', $where);
224: 
225:         $joins = "
226:             LEFT JOIN $t_rpu r ON f.rpuid = r.id
227:             LEFT JOIN $t_rp rp ON f.realpropertyid = rp.id
228:             LEFT JOIN $t_entity e ON f.taxpayer_objid = e.objid
229:             LEFT JOIN $t_txn tx ON f.txntype_objid = tx.code
230:         ";
231: 
232:         // Count
233:         $count_sql = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t_faas f $joins WHERE $where_sql";
234:         $total = !empty($values) ? $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare($count_sql, $values)) : $wpdb->get_var($count_sql);
235: 
236:         // Build ORDER BY
237:         $order_by = 'f.txntimestamp DESC';
238:         if (!empty($params['order_by'])) {
239:             $allowed = array('tdno', 'owner_name', 'total_assessed_value', 'created_at', 'effectivity_year', 'txntimestamp');
240:             $field = $params['order_by'];
241:             if ($field === 'created_at') $field = 'txntimestamp';
242:             
243:             if (in_array($field, $allowed)) {
244:                 $dir = (!empty($params['order_direction']) && strtoupper($params['order_direction']) === 'ASC') ? 'ASC' : 'DESC';
245:                 if ($field === 'total_assessed_value') {
246:                     $order_by = "r.$field $dir";
247:                 } else {
248:                     $order_by = "f.$field $dir";
249:                 }
250:             }

107:     public function delete_entity($id, $request) {
108:         global $wpdb;
109:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
110: 
111:         // Check if entity is used in any FAAS record
112:         $faas_table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
113:         $in_use = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $faas_table WHERE taxpayer_objid = %s AND state != 'DELETED'", $id));
114:         
115:         if ($in_use > 0) {
116:             return new WP_Error('entity_in_use', 'Cannot delete entity because it is used in active FAAS records', array('status' => 400));
117:         }
118: 
119:         $wpdb->delete($table, array('objid' => $id), array('%s'));
120:         return rest_ensure_response(array('success' => true, 'message' => 'Entity deleted successfully'));
121:     }

564:             'entity_name' => $name,
565:             'entity_address' => $address,
566:             'entity_type' => $type,
567:         ), array('%s', '%s', '%s'));

20: 
21:         $page = isset($params['page']) ? max(1, intval($params['page'])) : 1;
22:         $per_page = isset($params['per_page']) ? min(100, max(1, intval($params['per_page']))) : 20;
23:         $offset = ($page - 1) * $per_page;
24: 
25:         $where = array("1=1");
26:         $values = array();
27: 
28:         if (!empty($params['q'])) {
29:             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['q']) . '%';
30:             $where[] = "(e.name LIKE %s OR e.address_text LIKE %s)";
31:             $values[] = $like;
32:             $values[] = $like;
33:         }
34: 
35:         $where_sql = implode(' AND ', $where);
36:         $count_query = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $table e WHERE $where_sql";
37:         $total = !empty($values) ? $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare($count_query, $values)) : $wpdb->get_var($count_query);
38: 
39:         $sql = "SELECT e.*, e.name AS entity_name, e.address_text AS entity_address, e.type AS entity_type FROM $table e WHERE $where_sql ORDER BY e.name ASC LIMIT %d OFFSET %d";
40:         $query_values = array_merge($values, array($per_page, $offset));
41:         $results = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare($sql, $query_values));
42: 
43:         return rest_ensure_response(array(
44:             'data' => $results,
45:             'total' => intval($total),
46:             'page' => $page,
47:             'per_page' => $per_page,
48:             'total_pages' => ceil(intval($total) / $per_page)
49:         ));
50:     }
51: 
52:     public function create_entity($request) {
53:         global $wpdb;
54:         $params = $request->get_params();
55:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
56: 
57:         if (empty($params['entity_name'])) {
58:             return new WP_Error('missing_field', "Field 'entity_name' is required", array('status' => 400));
59:         }
60: 
61:         $objid = wp_generate_uuid4();
62:         
63:         $data = array(
64:             'objid' => $objid,
65:             'entityno' => 'ENT-' . time(), // Basic generation for now
66:             'name' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']),
67:             'entityname' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']),
68:             'address_text' => sanitize_textarea_field($params['entity_address'] ?? ''),
69:             'type' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_type'] ?? 'INDIVIDUAL'),
70:             'first_name' => sanitize_text_field($params['first_name'] ?? ''),
71:             'last_name' => sanitize_text_field($params['last_name'] ?? ''),
72:             'middle_name' => sanitize_text_field($params['middle_name'] ?? ''),
73:             'birthdate' => !empty($params['birthdate']) ? sanitize_text_field($params['birthdate']) : null,
74:             'birthplace' => sanitize_text_field($params['birthplace'] ?? ''),
75:             'gender' => sanitize_text_field($params['gender'] ?? ''),
76:             'civil_status' => sanitize_text_field($params['civil_status'] ?? ''),
77:             'citizenship' => sanitize_text_field($params['citizenship'] ?? ''),
78:             'profession' => sanitize_text_field($params['profession'] ?? ''),
79:             'tin' => sanitize_text_field($params['tin'] ?? ''),
80:             'sss' => sanitize_text_field($params['sss'] ?? ''),
81:             'acr' => sanitize_text_field($params['acr'] ?? ''),
82:             'religion' => sanitize_text_field($params['religion'] ?? ''),
83:             'height' => sanitize_text_field($params['height'] ?? ''),
84:             'weight' => sanitize_text_field($params['weight'] ?? ''),
85:             'date_registered' => !empty($params['date_registered']) ? sanitize_text_field($params['date_registered']) : null,
86:             'org_type' => sanitize_text_field($params['org_type'] ?? ''),
87:             'nature_of_business' => sanitize_text_field($params['nature_of_business'] ?? ''),
88:             'place_registered' => sanitize_text_field($params['place_registered'] ?? ''),
89:             'admin_name' => sanitize_text_field($params['admin_name'] ?? ''),
90:             'admin_position' => sanitize_text_field($params['admin_position'] ?? ''),
91:             'admin_address' => sanitize_text_field($params['admin_address'] ?? ''),
92:         );
93: 
94:         $result = $wpdb->insert($table, $data);
95:         if ($result === false) {
96:             return new WP_Error('insert_failed', 'Failed to create entity', array('status' => 500));
97:         }
98: 
99:         $entity = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare("SELECT e.*, e.name AS entity_name, e.address_text AS entity_address, e.type AS entity_type FROM $table e WHERE objid = %s", $objid));
100:         return rest_ensure_response($entity);
101:     }
102: 
103:     public function update_entity($id, $request) {
104:         global $wpdb;
105:         $params = $request->get_params();
106:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
107: 
108:         $data = array();
109:         if (isset($params['entity_name'])) {
110:             $data['name'] = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']);
111:             $data['entityname'] = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']);
112:         }
113:         if (isset($params['entity_address'])) {
114:             $data['address_text'] = sanitize_textarea_field($params['entity_address']);
115:         }
116:         if (isset($params['entity_type'])) {
117:             $data['type'] = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_type']);
118:         }
119:         
120:         $fields = ['first_name', 'last_name', 'middle_name', 'birthdate', 'birthplace', 'gender', 'civil_status', 'citizenship', 'profession', 'tin', 'sss', 'acr', 'religion', 'height', 'weight', 'date_registered', 'org_type', 'nature_of_business', 'place_registered', 'admin_name', 'admin_position', 'admin_address'];
121:         foreach ($fields as $f) {
122:             if (isset($params[$f])) {
123:                 $data[$f] = !empty($params[$f]) ? sanitize_text_field($params[$f]) : null;
124:             }
125:         }
126: 
127:         if (empty($data)) {
128:             return new WP_Error('no_data', 'No data provided to update', array('status' => 400));
129:         }
130: 
131:         $wpdb->update($table, $data, array('objid' => $id));
132:         $entity = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare("SELECT e.*, e.name AS entity_name, e.address_text AS entity_address, e.type AS entity_type FROM $table e WHERE objid = %s", $id));
133:         return rest_ensure_response($entity);
134:     }
135: 
136:     public function delete_entity($id, $request) {
137:         global $wpdb;
138:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
139: 
140:         // Check if entity is used in any FAAS record
141:         $faas_table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
142:         $in_use = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $faas_table WHERE taxpayer_objid = %s AND state != 'DELETED'", $id));
143:         
144:         if ($in_use > 0) {
145:             return new WP_Error('entity_in_use', 'Cannot delete entity because it is used in active FAAS records', array('status' => 400));
146:         }
147: 
148:         $wpdb->delete($table, array('objid' => $id), array('%s'));
149:         return rest_ensure_response(array('success' => true, 'message' => 'Entity deleted successfully'));
150:     }

155: 
156:     public function get_transaction_types($request) {
157:         global $wpdb;
158:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas_txntypes';
159:         $results = $wpdb->get_results("SELECT * FROM $table WHERE status = 'active' ORDER BY sort_order ASC, name ASC");
160:         return rest_ensure_response($results);
161:     }

163:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
164:     // FAAS LISTING (main list view)
165:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
166: 
167:     public function get_faas_list($request) {
168:         global $wpdb;
169:         $params = $request->get_params();
170: 
171:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
172:         $t_rpu = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_rpu';
173:         $t_rp = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_real_property';
174:         $t_entity = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
175:         $t_txn = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas_txntypes';
176: 
177:         $page = isset($params['page']) ? max(1, intval($params['page'])) : 1;
178:         $per_page = isset($params['per_page']) ? min(100, max(1, intval($params['per_page']))) : 20;
179:         $offset = ($page - 1) * $per_page;
180: 
181:         $where = array("f.state != 'DELETED'");
182:         $values = array();
183: 
184:         // Free-text search
185:         if (!empty($params['q'])) {
186:             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['q']) . '%';
187:             $where[] = "(f.tdno LIKE %s OR f.owner_name LIKE %s OR e.name LIKE %s OR rp.cadastral_lot_no LIKE %s OR f.fullpin LIKE %s)";
188:             $values = array_merge($values, array($like, $like, $like, $like, $like));
189:         }
190: 
191:         // Specific filters
192:         if (!empty($params['state'])) {
193:             $where[] = "f.state = %s";
194:             $values[] = strtoupper($params['state']);
195:         }
196:         if (!empty($params['rpu_type'])) {
197:             $where[] = "r.rpu_type = %s";
198:             $values[] = strtoupper($params['rpu_type']);
199:         }
200:         if (!empty($params['txntype'])) {
201:             $where[] = "f.txntype_objid = %s";
202:             $values[] = $params['txntype'];
203:         }
204:         if (!empty($params['barangay'])) {
205:             $where[] = "rp.barangay LIKE %s";
206:             $values[] = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['barangay']) . '%';
207:         }
208:         if (!empty($params['classification'])) {
209:             $where[] = "r.classification = %s";
210:             $values[] = $params['classification'];
211:         }
212:         if (!empty($params['tdno'])) {
213:             $where[] = "f.tdno LIKE %s";
214:             $values[] = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['tdno']) . '%';
215:         }
216:         if (!empty($params['owner_name'])) {
217:             $where[] = "(f.owner_name LIKE %s OR e.name LIKE %s)";
218:             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['owner_name']) . '%';
219:             $values[] = $like;
220:             $values[] = $like;
221:         }
222: 
223:         $where_sql = implode(' AND ', $where);
224: 
225:         $joins = "
226:             LEFT JOIN $t_rpu r ON f.rpuid = r.id
227:             LEFT JOIN $t_rp rp ON f.realpropertyid = rp.id
228:             LEFT JOIN $t_entity e ON f.taxpayer_objid = e.objid
229:             LEFT JOIN $t_txn tx ON f.txntype_objid = tx.code
230:         ";
231: 
232:         // Count
233:         $count_sql = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t_faas f $joins WHERE $where_sql";
234:         $total = !empty($values) ? $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare($count_sql, $values)) : $wpdb->get_var($count_sql);
235: 
236:         // Build ORDER BY
237:         $order_by = 'f.txntimestamp DESC';
238:         if (!empty($params['order_by'])) {
239:             $allowed = array('tdno', 'owner_name', 'total_assessed_value', 'created_at', 'effectivity_year', 'txntimestamp');
240:             $field = $params['order_by'];
241:             if ($field === 'created_at') $field = 'txntimestamp';
242:             
243:             if (in_array($field, $allowed)) {
244:                 $dir = (!empty($params['order_direction']) && strtoupper($params['order_direction']) === 'ASC') ? 'ASC' : 'DESC';
245:                 if ($field === 'total_assessed_value') {
246:                     $order_by = "r.$field $dir";
247:                 } else {
248:                     $order_by = "f.$field $dir";
249:                 }
250:             }
251:         }
252: 
253:         $select_sql = "
254:             SELECT
255:                 f.*,
256:                 f.objid AS id,
257:                 f.rpuid AS rpu_id,
258:                 f.realpropertyid AS real_property_id,
259:                 f.taxpayer_objid AS taxpayer_id,
260:                 f.effectivityyear AS effectivity_year,
261:                 f.effectivityqtr AS effectivity_qtr,
262:                 f.txntype_objid AS txntype_code,
263:                 f.titletype AS title_type,
264:                 f.titleno AS title_no,
265:                 f.titledate AS title_date,
266:                 f.dtapproved AS date_approved,
267:                 f.year AS year_issued,
268:                 f.cancelreason AS cancel_reason,
269:                 f.canceldate AS cancel_date,
270:                 f.cancelnote AS cancel_note,
271:                 f.prevowner AS prev_owner,
272:                 f.prevav AS prev_assessed_value,
273:                 f.prevmv AS prev_market_value,
274:                 f.prevareaha AS prev_area_hectare,
275:                 f.prevareasqm AS prev_area_sqm,
276:                 f.preveffectivity AS prev_effectivity,
277:                 f.cancelledyear AS cancelled_year,
278:                 f.cancelledbytdnos AS cancelled_by_tdnos,
279:                 (SELECT f2.fullpin FROM wp_assessor_faas f2 WHERE f2.tdno = f.cancelledbytdnos LIMIT 1) AS cancelled_by_pin,
280:                 r.rpu_type,
281:                 r.classification,
282:                 r.total_market_value,
283:                 r.total_assessed_value,
284:                 r.ry AS revision_year,
285:                 rp.pin AS rp_pin,
286:                 rp.cadastral_lot_no,
287:                 rp.survey_no,
288:                 rp.block_no,
289:                 rp.barangay,
290:                 r.total_area_hectare,
291:                 r.total_area_sqm,
292:                 e.name AS taxpayer_name,
293:                 e.address_text AS taxpayer_address,
294:                 tx.name AS txntype_name
295:             FROM $t_faas f
296:             $joins
297:             WHERE $where_sql
298:             ORDER BY $order_by
299:             LIMIT %d OFFSET %d
300:         ";
301: 
302:         $query_values = array_merge($values, array($per_page, $offset));
303:         $results = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare($select_sql, $query_values));
304: 
305:         return rest_ensure_response(array(
306:             'data' => $results,
307:             'total' => intval($total),
308:             'page' => $page,
309:             'per_page' => $per_page,
310:             'total_pages' => ceil(intval($total) / $per_page)
311:         ));
312:     }

314:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
315:     // FAAS SINGLE RECORD
316:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
317: 
318:     public function get_faas($id) {
319:         global $wpdb;
320: 
321:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
322:         $t_rpu = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_rpu';
323:         $t_rp = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_real_property';
324:         $t_entity = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
325:         $t_txn = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas_txntypes';
326: 
327:         $sql = "
328:             SELECT
329:                 f.*,
330:                 f.objid AS id,
331:                 f.rpuid AS rpu_id,
332:                 f.realpropertyid AS real_property_id,
333:                 f.taxpayer_objid AS taxpayer_id,
334:                 f.effectivityyear AS effectivity_year,
335:                 f.effectivityqtr AS effectivity_qtr,
336:                 f.txntype_objid AS txntype_code,
337:                 f.titletype AS title_type,
338:                 f.titleno AS title_no,
339:                 f.titledate AS title_date,
340:                 f.dtapproved AS date_approved,
341:                 f.year AS year_issued,
342:                 f.cancelreason AS cancel_reason,
343:                 f.canceldate AS cancel_date,
344:                 f.cancelnote AS cancel_note,
345:                 f.prevowner AS prev_owner,
346:                 f.prevav AS prev_assessed_value,
347:                 f.prevmv AS prev_market_value,
348:                 f.prevareaha AS prev_area_hectare,
349:                 f.prevareasqm AS prev_area_sqm,
350:                 f.preveffectivity AS prev_effectivity,
351:                 r.rpu_type,
352:                 r.classification,
353:                 r.total_market_value,
354:                 r.total_assessed_value,
355:                 r.ry AS revision_year,
356:                 rp.pin AS rp_pin,
357:                 rp.cadastral_lot_no,
358:                 rp.survey_no,
359:                 rp.block_no,
360:                 rp.barangay,
361:                 r.total_area_hectare,
362:                 r.total_area_sqm,
363:                 rp.north,
364:                 rp.south,
365:                 rp.east,
366:                 rp.west,
367:                 f.cancelledyear AS cancelled_year,
368:                 f.cancelledbytdnos AS cancelled_by_tdnos,
369:                 (SELECT f2.fullpin FROM wp_assessor_faas f2 WHERE f2.tdno = f.cancelledbytdnos LIMIT 1) AS cancelled_by_pin,
370:                 e.name AS taxpayer_name,
371:                 e.address_text AS taxpayer_address,
372:                 e.telephone_no AS taxpayer_telephone_no,
373:                 e.type AS taxpayer_type,
374:                 tx.name AS txntype_name
375:             FROM $t_faas f
376:             LEFT JOIN $t_rpu r ON f.rpuid = r.id
377:             LEFT JOIN $t_rp rp ON f.realpropertyid = rp.id
378:             LEFT JOIN $t_entity e ON f.taxpayer_objid = e.objid
379:             LEFT JOIN $t_txn tx ON f.txntype_objid = tx.code
380:             WHERE f.objid = %s
381:         ";
382: 
383:         $record = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare($sql, $id));
384:         if (!$record) {
385:             return new WP_Error('not_found', 'FAAS record not found', array('status' => 404));
386:         }
387: 
388:         return $record;
389:     }
390: 
391:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
392:     // FAAS CREATE
393:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
394: 
395:     public function create_faas($request) {
396:         global $wpdb;
397:         $params = $request->get_params();
398:         $auth = new Assessor_Auth();
399:         $user_id = $auth->get_user_id_from_token($request);
400: 
401:         // Validate required
402:         $required = array('tdno', 'owner_name');
403:         foreach ($required as $field) {
404:             if (empty($params[$field])) {
405:                 return new WP_Error('missing_field', "Field '$field' is required", array('status' => 400));
406:             }
407:         }
408: 
409:         // Check duplicate TD
410:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
411:         $existing = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
412:             "SELECT objid FROM $t_faas WHERE tdno = %s AND state != 'DELETED'",
413:             $params['tdno']
414:         ));
415:         if ($existing) {
416:             return new WP_Error('duplicate_tdno', 'Tax Declaration Number already exists', array('status' => 400));
417:         }
418: 
419:         // 1. Upsert Entity (taxpayer)
420:         $taxpayer_id = $this->upsert_entity($params);
421: 
422:         // 2. Create Real Property
423:         $real_property_id = $this->create_real_property($params);
424: 
425:         // 3. Create RPU
426:         $rpu_id = $this->create_rpu($params, $real_property_id);
427: 
428:         // 4. Create FAAS
429:         $faas_objid = wp_generate_uuid4();
430:         $faas_data = array(
431:             'objid'                 => $faas_objid,
432:             'state'                 => 'CURRENT',
433:             'rpuid'                 => $rpu_id,
434:             'realpropertyid'        => $real_property_id,
435:             'taxpayer_objid'        => $taxpayer_id,
436:             'tdno'                  => sanitize_text_field($params['tdno']),
437:             'utdno'                 => !empty($params['utdno']) ? sanitize_text_field($params['utdno']) : sanitize_text_field($params['tdno']),
438:             'txntype_objid'         => sanitize_text_field($params['txntype_code'] ?? 'GR'),
439:             'effectivityyear'       => intval($params['effectivity_year'] ?? 0),
440:             'effectivityqtr'        => intval($params['effectivity_qtr'] ?? 0),
441:             'owner_name'            => sanitize_text_field($params['owner_name']),
442:             'owner_address'         => sanitize_textarea_field($params['owner_address'] ?? ''),
443:             'administrator_name'    => sanitize_text_field($params['administrator_name'] ?? ''),
444:             'administrator_address' => sanitize_text_field($params['administrator_address'] ?? ''),
445:             'beneficiary_name'      => sanitize_text_field($params['beneficiary_name'] ?? ''),
446:             'beneficiary_address'   => sanitize_text_field($params['beneficiary_address'] ?? ''),
447:             'fullpin'               => sanitize_text_field($params['pin'] ?? ''),
448:             'titletype'             => sanitize_text_field($params['title_type'] ?? ''),
449:             'titleno'               => sanitize_text_field($params['title_no'] ?? ''),
450:             'titledate'             => !empty($params['title_date']) ? $params['title_date'] : null,
451:             'prevtdno'              => sanitize_text_field($params['prevtdno'] ?? ''),
452:             'prevowner'             => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_owner'] ?? ''),
453:             'prevav'                => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_assessed_value'] ?? ''),
454:             'prevmv'                => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_market_value'] ?? ''),
455:             'prevareaha'            => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_area_hectare'] ?? ''),
456:             'prevareasqm'           => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_area_sqm'] ?? ''),
457:             'preveffectivity'       => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_effectivity'] ?? ''),
458:             'memoranda'             => sanitize_textarea_field($params['memoranda'] ?? ''),
459:             'backtaxyrs'            => intval($params['back_tax_years'] ?? 0),
460:             'ryordinanceno'         => sanitize_text_field($params['ry_ordinance_no'] ?? ''),
461:             'ryordinancedate'       => !empty($params['ry_ordinance_date']) ? $params['ry_ordinance_date'] : null,
462:             'dtapproved'            => !empty($params['date_approved']) ? $params['date_approved'] : null,
463:             'year'                  => !empty($params['year_issued']) ? intval($params['year_issued']) : null,
464:             'publicland'            => intval($params['public_land'] ?? 0),
465:             'txntimestamp'          => current_time('mysql')
466:         );
467: 
468:         $result = $wpdb->insert($t_faas, $faas_data);
469:         if ($result === false) {
470:             return new WP_Error('insert_failed', 'Failed to create FAAS record: ' . $wpdb->last_error, array('status' => 500));
471:         }
472: 
473:         // Cancel previous TD if provided
474:         if (!empty($params['prevtdno'])) {
475:             $prev_td = sanitize_text_field($params['prevtdno']);
476:             $wpdb->query($wpdb->prepare(
477:                 "UPDATE $t_faas SET state = 'CANCELLED', canceldate = %s, cancelledtimestamp = %s, cancelledbytdnos = %s WHERE tdno = %s AND state != 'CANCELLED'",
478:                 current_time('mysql'),
479:                 current_time('mysql'),
480:                 $faas_data['tdno'],
481:                 $prev_td
482:             ));
483:         }
484: 
485:         return rest_ensure_response($this->get_faas($faas_objid));
486:     }
487: 
488:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
489:     // FAAS UPDATE
490:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
491: 
492:     public function update_faas($id, $request) {
493:         global $wpdb;
494:         $params = $request->get_params();
495:         $auth = new Assessor_Auth();
496:         $user_id = $auth->get_user_id_from_token($request);
497: 
498:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
499:         $current = $this->get_faas($id);
500:         if (is_wp_error($current)) return $current;
501: 
502:         // Check duplicate TD if changing
503:         if (isset($params['tdno'])) {
504:             $new_td = sanitize_text_field($params['tdno']);
505:             $dup = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
506:                 "SELECT objid FROM $t_faas WHERE tdno = %s AND objid != %s AND state != 'DELETED'",
507:                 $new_td,
508:                 $id
509:             ));
510:             if ($dup) {
511:                 return new WP_Error('duplicate_tdno', 'Tax Declaration Number already exists', array('status' => 400));
512:             }
513:         }

516:         if (isset($params['owner_name']) || isset($params['owner_address'])) {

527:             $this->update_rpu($current->rpu_id, $params);
528:         }
529: 
530:         // Map frontend fields to DB columns
531:         $field_map = array(
532:             'tdno'                  => 'tdno',
533:             'utdno'                 => 'utdno',
534:             'txntype_code'          => 'txntype_objid',
535:             'effectivity_year'      => 'effectivityyear',
536:             'effectivity_qtr'       => 'effectivityqtr',

564:             'cancel_date'           => 'canceldate',

566:         );
567: 
568:         $update_data = array();
569:         // Since ETRACS schema doesn't have an updated_by column, skip it.
570:         // Or if it does, add it. The schema given by user does not have updated_by.
571: 
572:         foreach ($field_map as $param_key => $db_col) {
573:             if (!array_key_exists($param_key, $params)) continue;
574:             
575:             $val = $params[$param_key];
576:             
577:             if (in_array($param_key, array('effectivity_year', 'effectivity_qtr', 'back_tax_years', 'public_land', 'year_issued'))) {
578:                 $update_data[$db_col] = !empty($val) ? intval($val) : 0;
579:             } elseif (in_array($param_key, array('title_date', 'ry_ordinance_date', 'date_approved', 'cancel_date'))) {
580:                 $update_data[$db_col] = !empty($val) ? $val : null;
581:             } else {
582:                 $update_data[$db_col] = sanitize_text_field($val);
583:             }
584:         }
585: 
586:         if (isset($update_data['utdno']) && empty($update_data['utdno'])) {
587:             $update_data['utdno'] = $update_data['tdno'] ?? $current->tdno;
588:         }
589: 
590:         $wpdb->update($t_faas, $update_data, array('objid' => $id));
591: 
592:         // Cancel previous TD if provided/updated
593:         if (!empty($params['prevtdno'])) {
594:             $prev_td = sanitize_text_field($params['prevtdno']);
595:             $new_td = $update_data['tdno'] ?? $current->tdno;
596:             $wpdb->query($wpdb->prepare(
597:                 "UPDATE $t_faas SET state = 'CANCELLED', canceldate = %s, cancelledtimestamp = %s, cancelledbytdnos = %s WHERE tdno = %s AND state != 'CANCELLED'",
598:                 current_time('mysql'),
599:                 current_time('mysql'),
600:                 $new_td,
601:                 $prev_td
602:             ));
603:         }
604: 
605:         return rest_ensure_response($this->get_faas($id));
606:     }

628:     public function cancel_faas($id, $request) {
629:         global $wpdb;
630:         $params = $request->get_params();
631:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
632: 
633:         $current = $this->get_faas($id);
634:         if (is_wp_error($current)) return $current;
635: 
636:         if ($current->state !== 'CURRENT') {
637:             return new WP_Error('invalid_state', 'Only CURRENT records can be cancelled', array('status' => 400));
638:         }
639: 
640:         $update = array(
641:             'state'             => 'CANCELLED',
642:             'cancelreason'      => sanitize_text_field($params['cancel_reason'] ?? ''),
643:             'canceldate'        => !empty($params['cancel_date']) ? $params['cancel_date'] : current_time('mysql'),
644:             'cancelnote'        => sanitize_text_field($params['cancel_note'] ?? ''),
645:             'cancelledbytdnos'  => sanitize_text_field($params['cancelled_by_tdnos'] ?? ''),
646:             'cancelledtimestamp'=> current_time('mysql'),
647:         );
648: 
649:         $wpdb->update($t_faas, $update, array('objid' => $id));
650:         return rest_ensure_response($this->get_faas($id));
651:     }

657:     public function get_faas_stats($request) {
