265:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
266:     // FAAS SINGLE RECORD
267:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
268: 
269:     public function get_faas($id) {
270:         global $wpdb;
271: 
272:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
273:         $t_rpu = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_rpu';
274:         $t_rp = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_real_property';
275:         $t_entity = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entities';
276:         $t_txn = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas_txntypes';
277: 
278:         $sql = "
279:             SELECT
280:                 f.*,
281:                 r.rpu_type,
282:                 r.classification,
283:                 r.total_market_value,
284:                 r.total_assessed_value,
285:                 r.ry AS revision_year,
286:                 rp.pin AS rp_pin,
287:                 rp.cadastral_lot_no,
288:                 rp.survey_no,
289:                 rp.block_no,
290:                 rp.barangay,
291:                 rp.total_area_hectare,
292:                 rp.total_area_sqm,
293:                 rp.north,
294:                 rp.south,
295:                 rp.east,
296:                 rp.west,
297:                 e.entity_name AS taxpayer_name,
298:                 e.entity_address AS taxpayer_address,
299:                 e.entity_type AS taxpayer_type,
300:                 tx.name AS txntype_name
301:             FROM $t_faas f
302:             LEFT JOIN $t_rpu r ON f.rpuid = r.id
303:             LEFT JOIN $t_rp rp ON f.realpropertyid = rp.id
304:             LEFT JOIN $t_entity e ON f.taxpayer_objid = e.objid
305:             LEFT JOIN $t_txn tx ON f.txntype_objid = tx.code
306:             WHERE f.objid = %s
307:         ";
308: 
309:         $record = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare($sql, $id));
310:         if (!$record) {
311:             return new WP_Error('not_found', 'FAAS record not found', array('status' => 404));
312:         }
313: 
314:         return $record;
315:     }

19:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
20: 
21:         $page = isset($params['page']) ? max(1, intval($params['page'])) : 1;

29:             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['q']) . '%';
30:             $where[] = "(e.name LIKE %s OR e.address_text LIKE %s)";
31:             $values[] = $like;
32:             $values[] = $like;
33:         }
34: 
35:         $where_sql = implode(' AND ', $where);
36:         $count_query = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $table e WHERE $where_sql";
37:         $total = !empty($values) ? $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare($count_query, $values)) : $wpdb->get_var($count_query);
38: 
39:         $sql = "SELECT e.*, e.name AS entity_name, e.address_text AS entity_address, e.type AS entity_type FROM $table e WHERE $where_sql ORDER BY e.name ASC LIMIT %d OFFSET %d";

41:         $results = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare($sql, $query_values));

535:     public function cancel_faas($id, $request) {
536:         global $wpdb;

19:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
20: 
21:         $page = isset($params['page']) ? max(1, intval($params['page'])) : 1;
22:         $per_page = isset($params['per_page']) ? min(100, max(1, intval($params['per_page']))) : 20;

24:

26:         $values = array();

32:             $values[] = $like;

34: 
35:         $where_sql = implode(' AND ', $where);

38:

41:         $results = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare($sql, $query_values));
42: 
43:         return rest_ensure_response(array(
44:             'data' => $results,
45:             'total' => intval($total),
46:             'page' => $page,
47:             'per_page' => $per_page,
48:             'total_pages' => ceil(intval($total) / $per_page)

46:             'page' => $page,
47:             'per_page' => $per_page,
48:             'total_pages' => ceil(intval($total) / $per_page)
49:         ));
50:     }
51: 
52:     public function create_entity($request) {
53:         global $wpdb;
54:         $params = $request->get_params();
55:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
56: 
57:         if (empty($params['entity_name'])) {
58:             return new WP_Error('missing_field', "Field 'entity_name' is required", array('status' => 400));
59:         }
60: 
61:         $objid = wp_generate_uuid4();
62:         
63:         $data = array(
64:             'objid' => $objid,
65:             'entityno' => 'ENT-' . time(), // Basic generation for now
66:             'name' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']),
67:             'entityname' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']),
68:             'address_text' => sanitize_textarea_field($params['entity_address'] ?? ''),
69:             'type' => sanitize_text_field($params['entity_type'] ?? 'INDIVIDUAL'),
70:         );
71: 
72:         $result = $wpdb->insert($table, $data);
73:         if ($result === false) {
74:             return new WP_Error('insert_failed', 'Failed to create entity', array('status' => 500));
75:         }
76: 
77:         $entity = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare("SELECT e.*, e.name AS entity_name, e.address_text AS entity_address, e.type AS entity_type FROM $table e WHERE objid = %s", $objid));
78:         return rest_ensure_response($entity);
79:     }
80: 
81:     public function update_entity($id, $request) {
82:         global $wpdb;
83:         $params = $request->get_params();
84:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
85: 
86:         $data = array();
87:         if (isset($params['entity_name'])) {
88:             $data['name'] = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']);
89:             $data['entityname'] = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_name']);
90:         }
91:         if (isset($params['entity_address'])) {
92:             $data['address_text'] = sanitize_textarea_field($params['entity_address']);
93:         }
94:         if (isset($params['entity_type'])) {
95:             $data['type'] = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_type']);
96:         }
97: 
98:         if (empty($data)) {
99:             return new WP_Error('no_data', 'No data provided to update', array('status' => 400));
100:         }
101: 
102:         $wpdb->update($table, $data, array('objid' => $id));
103:         $entity = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare("SELECT e.*, e.name AS entity_name, e.address_text AS entity_address, e.type AS entity_type FROM $table e WHERE objid = %s", $id));
104:         return rest_ensure_response($entity);
105:     }
106: 
107:     public function delete_entity($id, $request) {
108:         global $wpdb;
109:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
110: 
111:         // Check if entity is used in any FAAS record
112:         $faas_table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
113:         $in_use = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $faas_table WHERE taxpayer_objid = %s AND state != 'DELETED'", $id));
114:         
115:         if ($in_use > 0) {
116:             return new WP_Error('entity_in_use', 'Cannot delete entity because it is used in active FAAS records', array('status' => 400));
117:         }
118: 
119:         $wpdb->delete($table, array('objid' => $id), array('%s'));
120:         return rest_ensure_response(array('success' => true, 'message' => 'Entity deleted successfully'));
121:     }

123:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
124:     // TRANSACTION TYPES
125:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
126: 
127:     public function get_transaction_types($request) {
128:         global $wpdb;
129:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas_txntypes';
130:         $results = $wpdb->get_results("SELECT * FROM $table WHERE status = 'active' ORDER BY sort_order ASC, name ASC");
131:         return rest_ensure_response($results);
132:     }

134:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
135:     // FAAS LISTING (main list view)
136:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
137: 
138:     public function get_faas_list($request) {
139:         global $wpdb;
140:         $params = $request->get_params();
141: 
142:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
143:         $t_rpu = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_rpu';
144:         $t_rp = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_real_property';
145:         $t_entity = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entities';
146:         $t_txn = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas_txntypes';
147: 
148:         $page = isset($params['page']) ? max(1, intval($params['page'])) : 1;
149:         $per_page = isset($params['per_page']) ? min(100, max(1, intval($params['per_page']))) : 20;
150:         $offset = ($page - 1) * $per_page;
151: 
152:         $where = array("f.state != 'DELETED'");
153:         $values = array();
154: 
155:         // Free-text search
156:         if (!empty($params['q'])) {
157:             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['q']) . '%';
158:             $where[] = "(f.tdno LIKE %s OR f.owner_name LIKE %s OR e.entity_name LIKE %s OR rp.cadastral_lot_no LIKE %s OR f.pin LIKE %s)";
159:             $values = array_merge($values, array($like, $like, $like, $like, $like));
160:         }
161: 
162:         // Specific filters
163:         if (!empty($params['state'])) {
164:             $where[] = "f.state = %s";
165:             $values[] = strtoupper($params['state']);
166:         }
167:         if (!empty($params['rpu_type'])) {
168:             $where[] = "r.rpu_type = %s";
169:             $values[] = strtoupper($params['rpu_type']);
170:         }
171:         if (!empty($params['txntype'])) {
172:             $where[] = "f.txntype_code = %s";
173:             $values[] = $params['txntype'];
174:         }
175:         if (!empty($params['barangay'])) {
176:             $where[] = "rp.barangay LIKE %s";
177:             $values[] = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['barangay']) . '%';
178:         }
179:         if (!empty($params['classification'])) {
180:             $where[] = "r.classification = %s";
181:             $values[] = $params['classification'];
182:         }
183:         if (!empty($params['tdno'])) {
184:             $where[] = "f.tdno LIKE %s";
185:             $values[] = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['tdno']) . '%';
186:         }
187:         if (!empty($params['owner_name'])) {
188:             $where[] = "(f.owner_name LIKE %s OR e.name LIKE %s)";
189:             $like = '%' . $wpdb->esc_like($params['owner_name']) . '%';
190:             $values[] = $like;
191:             $values[] = $like;
192:         }
193: 
194:         $where_sql = implode(' AND ', $where);
195: 
196:         $joins = "
197:             LEFT JOIN $t_rpu r ON f.rpuid = r.id
198:             LEFT JOIN $t_rp rp ON f.realpropertyid = rp.id
199:             LEFT JOIN $t_entity e ON f.taxpayer_objid = e.objid
200:             LEFT JOIN $t_txn tx ON f.txntype_objid = tx.id
201:         ";
202: 
203:         // Count
204:         $count_sql = "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t_faas f $joins WHERE $where_sql";
205:         $total = !empty($values) ? $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare($count_sql, $values)) : $wpdb->get_var($count_sql);
206: 
207:         // Build ORDER BY
208:         $order_by = 'f.created_at DESC';
209:         if (!empty($params['order_by'])) {
210:             $allowed = array('tdno', 'owner_name', 'total_assessed_value', 'created_at', 'effectivity_year');
211:             $field = $params['order_by'];
212:             if (in_array($field, $allowed)) {
213:                 $dir = (!empty($params['order_direction']) && strtoupper($params['order_direction']) === 'ASC') ? 'ASC' : 'DESC';
214:                 $order_by = "f.$field $dir";
215:             }
216:         }
217: 
218:         $select_sql = "
219:             SELECT
220:                 f.*,
221:                 f.objid AS id,
222:                 f.rpuid AS rpu_id,
223:                 f.realpropertyid AS real_property_id,
224:                 f.taxpayer_objid AS taxpayer_id,
225:                 f.effectivityyear AS effectivity_year,
226:                 f.effectivityqtr AS effectivity_qtr,
227:                 f.txntype_objid AS txntype_code,
228:                 f.titletype AS title_type,
229:                 f.titleno AS title_no,
230:                 f.titledate AS title_date,
231:                 r.rpu_type,
232:                 r.classification,
233:                 r.total_market_value,
234:                 r.total_assessed_value,
235:                 r.ry AS revision_year,
236:                 rp.pin AS rp_pin,
237:                 rp.cadastral_lot_no,
238:                 rp.survey_no,
239:                 rp.block_no,
240:                 rp.barangay,
241:                 rp.total_area_hectare,
242:                 rp.total_area_sqm,
243:                 e.name AS taxpayer_name,
244:                 e.address_text AS taxpayer_address,
245:                 tx.name AS txntype_name
246:             FROM $t_faas f
247:             $joins
248:             WHERE $where_sql
249:             ORDER BY $order_by
250:             LIMIT %d OFFSET %d
251:         ";
252: 
253:         $query_values = array_merge($values, array($per_page, $offset));
254:         $results = $wpdb->get_results($wpdb->prepare($select_sql, $query_values));
255: 
256:         return rest_ensure_response(array(
257:             'data' => $results,
258:             'total' => intval($total),
259:             'page' => $page,
260:             'per_page' => $per_page,
261:             'total_pages' => ceil(intval($total) / $per_page)
262:         ));
263:     }
264: 
265:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
266:     // FAAS SINGLE RECORD
267:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
268: 
269:     public function get_faas($id) {
270:         global $wpdb;
271: 
272:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
273:         $t_rpu = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_rpu';
274:         $t_rp = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_real_property';
275:         $t_entity = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entities';
276:         $t_txn = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas_txntypes';
277: 
278:         $sql = "
279:             SELECT
280:                 f.*,
281:                 r.rpu_type,
282:                 r.classification,
283:                 r.total_market_value,
284:                 r.total_assessed_value,
285:                 r.ry AS revision_year,
286:                 rp.pin AS rp_pin,
287:                 rp.cadastral_lot_no,
288:                 rp.survey_no,
289:                 rp.block_no,
290:                 rp.barangay,
291:                 rp.total_area_hectare,
292:                 rp.total_area_sqm,
293:                 rp.north,
294:                 rp.south,
295:                 rp.east,
296:                 rp.west,
297:                 e.entity_name AS taxpayer_name,
298:                 e.entity_address AS taxpayer_address,
299:                 e.entity_type AS taxpayer_type,
300:                 tx.name AS txntype_name
301:             FROM $t_faas f
302:             LEFT JOIN $t_rpu r ON f.rpu_id = r.id
303:             LEFT JOIN $t_rp rp ON f.real_property_id = rp.id
304:             LEFT JOIN $t_entity e ON f.taxpayer_id = e.id
305:             LEFT JOIN $t_txn tx ON f.txntype_code = tx.code
306:             WHERE f.id = %d
307:         ";
308: 
309:         $record = $wpdb->get_row($wpdb->prepare($sql, $id));
310:         if (!$record) {
311:             return new WP_Error('not_found', 'FAAS record not found', array('status' => 404));
312:         }
313: 
314:         return $record;
315:     }
316: 
317:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
318:     // FAAS CREATE
319:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
320: 
321:     public function create_faas($request) {
322:         global $wpdb;
323:         $params = $request->get_params();
324:         $auth = new Assessor_Auth();
325:         $user_id = $auth->get_user_id_from_token($request);
326: 
327:         // Validate required
328:         $required = array('tdno', 'owner_name');
329:         foreach ($required as $field) {
330:             if (empty($params[$field])) {
331:                 return new WP_Error('missing_field', "Field '$field' is required", array('status' => 400));
332:             }
333:         }
334: 
335:         // Check duplicate TD
336:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
337:         $existing = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
338:             "SELECT id FROM $t_faas WHERE tdno = %s AND state != 'DELETED'",
339:             $params['tdno']
340:         ));
341:         if ($existing) {
342:             return new WP_Error('duplicate_tdno', 'Tax Declaration Number already exists', array('status' => 400));
343:         }
344: 
345:         // 1. Upsert Entity (taxpayer)
346:         $taxpayer_id = $this->upsert_entity($params);
347: 
348:         // 2. Create Real Property
349:         $real_property_id = $this->create_real_property($params);
350: 
351:         // 3. Create RPU
352:         $rpu_id = $this->create_rpu($params, $real_property_id);
353: 
354:         // 4. Create FAAS
355:         $faas_objid = wp_generate_uuid4();
356:         $faas_data = array(
357:             'objid'                 => $faas_objid,
358:             'state'                 => 'CURRENT',
359:             'rpuid'                 => $rpu_id,
360:             'realpropertyid'        => $real_property_id,
361:             'taxpayer_objid'        => $taxpayer_id,
362:             'tdno'                  => sanitize_text_field($params['tdno']),
363:             'utdno'                 => sanitize_text_field($params['utdno'] ?? $params['tdno']),
364:             'txntype_objid'         => sanitize_text_field($params['txntype_code'] ?? 'GR'),
365:             'effectivityyear'       => intval($params['effectivity_year'] ?? 0),
366:             'effectivityqtr'        => intval($params['effectivity_qtr'] ?? 0),
367:             'owner_name'            => sanitize_text_field($params['owner_name']),
368:             'owner_address'         => sanitize_textarea_field($params['owner_address'] ?? ''),
369:             'administrator_name'    => sanitize_text_field($params['administrator_name'] ?? ''),
370:             'administrator_address' => sanitize_text_field($params['administrator_address'] ?? ''),
371:             'beneficiary_name'      => sanitize_text_field($params['beneficiary_name'] ?? ''),
372:             'beneficiary_address'   => sanitize_text_field($params['beneficiary_address'] ?? ''),
373:             'fullpin'               => sanitize_text_field($params['pin'] ?? ''),
374:             'titletype'             => sanitize_text_field($params['title_type'] ?? ''),
375:             'titleno'               => sanitize_text_field($params['title_no'] ?? ''),
376:             'titledate'             => !empty($params['title_date']) ? $params['title_date'] : null,
377:             'prevtdno'              => sanitize_text_field($params['prevtdno'] ?? ''),
378:             'prevowner'             => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_owner'] ?? ''),
379:             'prevav'                => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_assessed_value'] ?? ''),
380:             'prevmv'                => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_market_value'] ?? ''),
381:             'prevareaha'            => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_area_hectare'] ?? ''),
382:             'prevareasqm'           => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_area_sqm'] ?? ''),
383:             'preveffectivity'       => sanitize_text_field($params['prev_effectivity'] ?? ''),
384:             'memoranda'             => sanitize_textarea_field($params['memoranda'] ?? ''),
385:             'backtaxyrs'            => intval($params['back_tax_years'] ?? 0),
386:             'ryordinanceno'         => sanitize_text_field($params['ry_ordinance_no'] ?? ''),
387:             'ryordinancedate'       => !empty($params['ry_ordinance_date']) ? $params['ry_ordinance_date'] : null,
388:             'dtapproved'            => !empty($params['date_approved']) ? $params['date_approved'] : null,
389:             'year'                  => !empty($params['year_issued']) ? intval($params['year_issued']) : null,
390:             'publicland'            => intval($params['public_land'] ?? 0),
391:             'txntimestamp'          => current_time('mysql')
392:         );
393: 
394:         $result = $wpdb->insert($t_faas, $faas_data);
395:         if ($result === false) {
396:             return new WP_Error('insert_failed', 'Failed to create FAAS record: ' . $wpdb->last_error, array('status' => 500));
397:         }
398: 
399:         $faas_id = $faas_objid;
400:         return rest_ensure_response($this->get_faas($faas_id));
401:     }
402: 
403:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
404:     // FAAS UPDATE
405:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
406: 
407:     public function update_faas($id, $request) {
408:         global $wpdb;
409:         $params = $request->get_params();
410:         $auth = new Assessor_Auth();
411:         $user_id = $auth->get_user_id_from_token($request);
412: 
413:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
414:         $current = $this->get_faas($id);
415:         if (is_wp_error($current)) return $current;
416: 
417:         // Check duplicate TD if changing
418:         if (isset($params['tdno'])) {
419:             $new_td = sanitize_text_field($params['tdno']);
420:             $dup = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
421:                 "SELECT id FROM $t_faas WHERE tdno = %s AND id != %d AND state != 'DELETED'",
422:                 $new_td,
423:                 $id
424:             ));
425:             if ($dup) {
426:                 return new WP_Error('duplicate_tdno', 'Tax Declaration Number already exists', array('status' => 400));
427:             }
428:         }
429: 
430:         // Update Entity if name/address changed
431:         if (isset($params['owner_name']) || isset($params['owner_address'])) {
432:             $this->upsert_entity($params, $current->taxpayer_id);
433:         }
434: 
435:         // Update Real Property
436:         if ($current->real_property_id) {
437:             $this->update_real_property($current->real_property_id, $params);
438:         }
439: 
440:         // Update RPU
441:         if ($current->rpu_id) {
442:             $this->update_rpu($current->rpu_id, $params);
443:         }
444: 
445:         // Update FAAS fields
446:         $faas_fields = array(
447:             'tdno', 'utdno', 'txntype_code', 'effectivity_year', 'effectivity_qtr',
448:             'owner_name', 'owner_address', 'administrator_name', 'administrator_address',
449:             'beneficiary_name', 'beneficiary_address', 'pin', 'title_type', 'title_no',
450:             'prevtdno', 'prev_owner', 'prev_assessed_value', 'prev_market_value',
451:             'prev_area_hectare', 'prev_area_sqm', 'prev_effectivity', 'memoranda',
452:             'back_tax_years', 'ry_ordinance_no', 'public_land', 'state',
453:             'cancel_reason', 'cancel_note', 'cancelled_by_tdnos'
454:         );
455: 
456:         $update_data = array('updated_by' => $user_id);
457:         foreach ($faas_fields as $f) {
458:             if (!array_key_exists($f, $params)) continue;
459:             if (in_array($f, array('effectivity_year', 'effectivity_qtr', 'back_tax_years', 'public_land', 'year_issued'))) {
460:                 $update_data[$f] = intval($params[$f]);
461:             } else {
462:                 $update_data[$f] = sanitize_text_field($params[$f]);
463:             }
464:         }
465: 
466:         // Date fields
467:         foreach (array('title_date', 'ry_ordinance_date', 'date_approved', 'cancel_date') as $df) {
468:             if (array_key_exists($df, $params)) {
469:                 $update_data[$df] = !empty($params[$df]) ? $params[$df] : null;
470:             }
471:         }
472:         if (array_key_exists('year_issued', $params)) {
473:             $update_data['year_issued'] = !empty($params['year_issued']) ? intval($params['year_issued']) : null;
474:         }
475: 
476:         $wpdb->update($t_faas, $update_data, array('id' => $id));
477: 
478:         return rest_ensure_response($this->get_faas($id));
479:     }
480: 
481:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
482:     // FAAS DELETE (soft delete → DELETED state)
483:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
484: 
485:     public function delete_faas($id, $request) {
486:         global $wpdb;
487:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
488: 
489:         $current = $this->get_faas($id);
490:         if (is_wp_error($current)) return $current;
491: 
492:         $wpdb->update($t_faas, array('state' => 'DELETED'), array('id' => $id));
493: 
494:         return rest_ensure_response(array('success' => true, 'message' => 'FAAS record deleted'));
495:     }
496: 
497:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
498:     // FAAS CANCEL
499:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
500: 
501:     public function cancel_faas($id, $request) {
502:         global $wpdb;
503:         $params = $request->get_params();
504:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
505: 
506:         $current = $this->get_faas($id);
507:         if (is_wp_error($current)) return $current;
508: 
509:         if ($current->state !== 'CURRENT') {
510:             return new WP_Error('invalid_state', 'Only CURRENT records can be cancelled', array('status' => 400));
511:         }
512: 
513:         $update = array(
514:             'state'             => 'CANCELLED',
515:             'cancel_reason'     => sanitize_text_field($params['cancel_reason'] ?? ''),
516:             'cancel_date'       => !empty($params['cancel_date']) ? $params['cancel_date'] : date('Y-m-d'),
517:             'cancel_note'       => sanitize_text_field($params['cancel_note'] ?? ''),
518:             'cancelled_by_tdnos'=> sanitize_text_field($params['cancelled_by_tdnos'] ?? ''),
519:         );
520: 
521:         $wpdb->update($t_faas, $update, array('id' => $id));
522:         return rest_ensure_response($this->get_faas($id));
523:     }
524: 
525:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
526:     // DASHBOARD / STATS
527:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
528: 
529:     public function get_faas_stats($request) {
530:         global $wpdb;
531:         $t_faas = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_faas';
532:         $t_rpu = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_rpu';
533: 
534:         $total = $wpdb->get_var("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t_faas WHERE state != 'DELETED'");
535:         $current = $wpdb->get_var("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t_faas WHERE state = 'CURRENT'");
536:         $cancelled = $wpdb->get_var("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM $t_faas WHERE state = 'CANCELLED'");
537: 
538:         $by_type = $wpdb->get_results("
539:             SELECT r.rpu_type, COUNT(*) as count
540:             FROM $t_faas f
541:             LEFT JOIN $t_rpu r ON f.rpu_id = r.id
542:             WHERE f.state != 'DELETED'
543:             GROUP BY r.rpu_type
544:         ");
545: 
546:         return rest_ensure_response(array(
547:             'total' => intval($total),
548:             'current' => intval($current),
549:             'cancelled' => intval($cancelled),
550:             'by_type' => $by_type
551:         ));
552:     }
553: 
554:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
555:     // PRIVATE HELPERS
556:     // ──────────────────────────────────────────────
557: 
558:     private function upsert_entity($params, $existing_id = null) {
559:         global $wpdb;
560:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_entity';
561: 
562:         $name = sanitize_text_field($params['owner_name'] ?? $params['entity_name'] ?? '');
563:         $address = sanitize_textarea_field($params['owner_address'] ?? $params['entity_address'] ?? '');
564:         $type = sanitize_text_field($params['entity_type'] ?? 'INDIVIDUAL');
565: 
566:         if ($existing_id) {
567:             $wpdb->update($table, array(
568:                 'name' => $name,
569:                 'entityname' => $name,
570:                 'address_text' => $address,
571:             ), array('objid' => $existing_id));
572:             return $existing_id;
573:         }
574: 
575:         // Try to find by exact name match
576:         $found = $wpdb->get_var($wpdb->prepare(
577:             "SELECT objid FROM $table WHERE entityname = %s LIMIT 1",
578:             $name
579:         ));
580:         if ($found) return $found;
581: 
582:         // Create new
583:         $objid = wp_generate_uuid4();
584:         $wpdb->insert($table, array(
585:             'objid' => $objid,
586:             'entityno' => 'ENT-' . time(),
587:             'name' => $name,
588:             'entityname' => $name,
589:             'address_text' => $address,
590:             'type' => $type,
591:         ));
592: 
593:         return $objid;
594:     }

621:         global $wpdb;
622:         $table = $wpdb->prefix . 'assessor_real_property';
623: 
624:         $fields = array('pin', 'cadastral_lot_no', 'survey_no', 'block_no', 'barangay', 'municipality', 'province', 'north', 'south', 'east', 'west');
625:         $update = array();
626:         foreach ($fields as $f) {
627:             // Map alternative field names
628:             $param_key = $f;
629:             if ($f === 'cadastral_lot_no' && !isset($params[$f]) && isset($params['lot_number'])) $param_key = 'lot_number';
630:             if (isset($params[$param_key])) {
631:                 $update[$f] = sanitize_text_field($params[$param_key]);
632:             }
633:         }
634:         // Numeric area fields
635:         foreach (array('total_area_hectare', 'total_area_sqm') as $af) {
636:             if (array_key_exists($af, $params)) {
637:                 $update[$af] = $params[$af] !== '' && $params[$af] !== null ? floatval($params[$af]) : null;
638:             }
639:         }
640: 
641:         if (!empty($update)) {
642:             $wpdb->update($table, $update, array('id' => $id));
643:         }
644:     }

578:             $name

580:         if ($found) return $found;

586:             'entityno' => 'ENT-' . time(),

589:             'address_text' => $address,
590:             'type' => $type,
591:         ));
592: 
593:         return $objid;
594:     }

638:             }
639:         }
640: 
641:         if (!empty($update)) {
642:             $wpdb->update($table, $update, array('id' => $id));
